{"antonyms":[{"sense":"antonym(s) of “overflow”","word":"drain"}],"forms":[{"form":"floods","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"flooding","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"flooded","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"flooded","tags":["past"]},{"form":"floud","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"People flooded into the streets in protest.","type":"example"},{"text":"The basement flooded with rainwater.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To overflow, as by water from excessive rainfall."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The floor was flooded with beer.","type":"example"},{"text":"They flooded the room with sewage.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To cover or partly fill as if by a flood."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The station's switchboard was flooded with listeners making complaints.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To provide (someone or something) with a larger number or quantity of something than can easily be dealt with."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"glosses":["To paste numerous lines of text to (a chat system) in order to disrupt the conversation."],"tags":["Internet","ambitransitive"]},{"glosses":["To bleed profusely, as after childbirth."]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"flŭd"},{"ipa":"/flʌd/"},{"enpr":"flo͝od","tags":["Northern-England"]},{"ipa":"/flʊd/","tags":["Northern-England"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"overflow","word":"overfill"},{"sense":"cover","word":"inundate"},{"sense":"provide with large number","word":"inundate"},{"sense":"provide with large number","word":"swamp"},{"sense":"provide with large number","word":"deluge"},{"sense":"overwhelm a chatroom or forum","word":"spam"}],"upstream_line":151547,"upstream_offset":689491551,"upstream_record_sha256":"853e8c30e76d4bc2b41a68f477a61f691add5d3b18634eed643dd257c1f0db10","word":"flood"}
